*Emmanuel Stefanakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探索了在密集城市环境下，基于高分辨率DEM和DSM数据，对比分析型山体阴影与基于神经网络的Eduard系统（原为模拟瑞士风格山体阴影设计）在山体阴影生成中的表现，重点考察参数调优能否使针对山地训练的网络在城市环境中产生视觉良好且具有制图价值的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分析型山体阴影方法在城市环境中的表现研究相对成熟，但神经网络方法（如Eduard）最初是针对山地景观训练的，尚未充分检验其在密集城市建成环境中的适应性。作者希望通过探索性比较，识别网络方法在城市地形表达中的优势、劣势及失败原因，为未来针对城市地形制图的模型训练和评估提供依据。

Method: 研究以加拿大卡尔加里市中心为研究区域，使用高分辨率DEM和DSM数据，对单方向、多方向分析型山体阴影与Eduard神经网络系统生成的浮雕阴影进行比较。实验重点考察地形类型、微观与宏观概括、平坦区域细节等参数对结果的影响，同时保持神经实验中的大尺度阴影风格恒定。研究采用探索性对比而非基准测试的方式。

Result: 研究识别出分析型山体阴影在城市环境中仍然更为可靠的场景，也发现了Eduard在部分情况下表现出的意外优势，以及由于神经网络对高山地形的训练偏差而导致在城市要素（建筑、桥梁、街道、树木等）表达上的失败案例。

Conclusion: 通过参数调优，Eduard可以在城市环境中产生视觉上具有吸引力且具有制图价值的山体阴影结果，部分情况下甚至优于传统分析型方法。未来需要专门针对城市浮雕阴影进行模型训练和评估，以克服当前方法对自然地形训练偏差的限制。

Abstract: This article explores the performance of analytical and neural-based hillshading methods in a dense urban environment using high-resolution digital elevation model (DEM) and digital surface model (DSM) data for downtown Calgary. The study compares single-direction and multi-direction analytical hillshading with relief shading generated in Eduard, a machine-learning system originally developed to emulate Swiss-style shaded relief trained primarily on mountainous landscapes. Because Eduard was not designed for buildings, bridges, streets, trees, and other urban infrastructures, the central question is not whether it perfectly reproduces urban morphology, but whether parameter tuning can nevertheless produce visually strong, cartographically useful, and in some cases superior results when compared with conventional analytical methods. The analysis focuses especially on terrain type, micro and macro generalization, and flat-area detail parameters, while keeping the large-scale shading style constant throughout the neural experiments. The article is structured as an exploratory comparison rather than a benchmark of universal best practice. It aims to identify where analytical hillshading remains more reliable, where Eduard offers unexpected strengths, and where neural shading fails because of its training bias toward alpine terrain. The study contributes to current work on terrain representation by testing whether a neural approach designed for natural landforms can be adapted to a highly built urban setting, and it concludes by arguing for future model training and evaluation specifically targeted at urban relief shading.

</details>


### [57] [Ask Self, Ask Others: Relation Is All You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20172)
*Yuting Ge,Pengju Yang,Mingkai Nie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Relation，一种替代注意力机制的token混合方法，先组织成显式的Self和Exchange关系再推导信息流，在多个模型规模上超越MHA性能，并在吞吐量上接近FlashAttention。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力机制直接从成对分数推导归一化信息流，忽视了中间的关系组织阶段。作者认为先显式组织关系、再推导信息流可能更有效。

Method: 提出Relation原语，将成对证据组织为Self和Exchange两类显式关系，再基于这些关系推导信息流。衍生出Full Relation、FlashRelation（高效实现）、Linear Relation（线性变体）、Hybrid Relation（混合模型）以及KV风格的Relation Cache。

Result: 在约10M、30M、100M参数量的解码器模型上，Full Relation在最终验证NLL上均低于MHA。FlashRelation比materialized的Full Relation实现快3.60-4.41倍，达到PyTorch FlashAttention吞吐量的76.4-84.9%。Hybrid Relation（75%线性层）在语言建模质量上表现优异。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持"关系优先"的token混合观点：先问Self，再问Others，然后让Flow跟随Relation，为设计新型token混合机制提供了新方向。

Abstract: Attention directly derives normalized information flow from pairwise scores. We introduce Relation, an alternative token-mixing primitive that first organizes pairwise evidence into explicit Self and Exchange relations and derives information flow afterward. This relational organization gives rise to Full Relation, FlashRelation, Linear Relation, Hybrid Relation, and a KV-style Relation Cache. Across matched decoder-only models at approximately 10M, 30M, and 100M parameters, Full Relation achieves lower final validation NLL than MHA at all three scales. In a fixed-context reference benchmark, FlashRelation is 3.60-4.41x faster than the materialized Full Relation implementation. Across scale-matched production workloads, it reaches 76.4-84.9% of PyTorch FlashAttention throughput while executing the Full Relation operator. Hybrid Relation uses 75% Linear Relation layers and achieves strong language-modeling quality. These results support a relation-first view of token mixing: ask Self, ask Others, then let Flow follow Relation.

</details>


### [58] [A Standardized Framework for Machine Learning in Power System Protection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20181)
*Julian Oelhaf,Georg Kordowich,Paula Andrea Pérez-Toro,Christian Bergler,Johann Jäger,Andreas Maier,Siming Bayer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向机器学习电力系统保护评估的标准化框架，定义七个必需的研究维度，并在PROTECT-90电磁暂态基准上进行了实证研究，揭示了评估设置对性能指标含义的重大影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于机器学习的电力系统保护研究普遍报告接近完美的性能分数，但这些分数的含义高度依赖评估设置，而保护任务、物理范围、测量、时序、目标、预处理和验证方式往往联合变化且描述不完整，导致研究结果难以比较、难以审计。

Method: 提出标准化评估框架，定义七个研究维度：保护目标、物理范围、可观测性、时序与决策窗口、目标与样本有效性、验证协议、评估输出。在PROTECT-90电磁暂态基准上（9022个模拟场景，90kV双线路拓扑），针对故障分类与定位任务，采用集中式感知、20ms仿真元数据对齐窗口、场景分组验证等设置进行实例化研究。

Result: 多层感知机MLP在五折交叉验证中，故障分类的宏平均F1分数为0.991±0.001，定位的平均绝对误差为线路长度的10.20±0.25%。决策窗口延长至50ms保持任务依赖的性能不对称性；降低可观测性使定位误差约翻倍但分类性能几乎不受影响；同步双端传统定位器在更丰富的信息集下优于学习型定位器；测量退化表明清洁条件下的预测性能不能决定鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该框架将评估假设转化为显式、可复现的证据，为机器学习保护功能的更可比、可审计的评估以及未来面向认证的评估提供了基础。

Abstract: Studies of machine-learning-based power-system protection increasingly report near-perfect scores, yet the meaning of those scores depends strongly on the evaluation setting. Protection task, physical scope, measurements, timing, targets, preprocessing, and validation often vary jointly and remain incompletely specified. This paper proposes a standardization-oriented framework that treats evaluation design as part of the scientific contribution. It defines seven required study dimensions: protection objective, physical scope, observability, timing and decision windows, targets and sample validity, validation protocol, and evaluation outputs. The framework is instantiated in a bounded case study on the public PROTECT-90 electromagnetic-transient benchmark, comprising 9022 simulated episodes from a 90 kV double-line topology, for onset-conditioned fault classification and localization. Under centralized sensing, simulation-metadata-aligned 20 ms windows, and episode-grouped validation, a multi-layer perceptron (MLP) achieved a five-fold mean macro-averaged F1 score of 0.991 +/- 0.001 for classification and a localization mean absolute error of 10.20 +/- 0.25% of line length (mean +/- std across episode-grouped folds). Extending the decision horizon to 50 ms preserved this task-dependent performance asymmetry, while reduced observability approximately doubled the MLP localization error but had little effect on classification. A synchronized two-ended conventional locator outperformed the learning locators under its richer clean information set, and measurement degradation showed that clean predictive performance did not determine robustness. The framework turns evaluation assumptions into explicit, reproducible evidence and provides a basis for more comparable, auditable evaluation and future certification-oriented assessment of machine-learning protection functions.

</details>


### [59] [Exact Algebraic Computation of Learning Coefficients for Two-Dimensional Singular Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20183)
*Grégoire Sergeant-Perthuis,Elias Tsigaridas,Jules Tsukahara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了第一个确定性算法，能够精确计算任意二维奇异模型的局部实对数典型阈值（RLCT），该算法基于Kullback-Leibler散度与多项式的接触等价性，并给出了复杂度界，超越传统仅依赖采样的估计方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典信息准则（如BIC）在奇异模型（如深度学习）中会失效，因为其正则性假设不成立。WBIC虽然通过局部学习系数λ（解析情况下等同于局部RLCT）来捕捉正确的边际似然渐近行为，但学习系数的精确计算仅限于特殊情况，一般只能依靠采样估计方法，缺乏精确的确定性计算手段。

Method: 作者提出了第一个确定性算法，用于精确计算任意二维模型（其KL散度接触等价于多项式）的局部RLCT。该方法推导了算法的复杂度上界，并在包括多项式神经网络在内的广泛模型类别上验证了其有效性。

Result: 该算法能为采样估计器提供真实基准（ground truth），并能揭示采样方法无法捕捉到的学习系数中的代数结构。在浅层（shallow）模型场景下，该算法的速度也优于采样方法。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了奇异模型学习系数精确计算的空白，为模型选择和信息准则的应用提供了新的确定性工具，兼具理论价值和实践优势。

Abstract: Classical information criteria such as the Bayesian Information Criterion (BIC) rely on regularity assumptions that break down for singular models, leading to incorrect model selection in settings such as deep learning. The Widely Applicable Bayesian Information Criterion (WBIC) relies on local learning coefficients $λ$, which in the analytic case coincides with local Real Log Canonical Thresholds (RLCT) of the Kullback-Leibler divergence of the model, to capture correct marginal likelihood asymptotics. Exact computation of the learning coefficients has been limited to special cases, and only sampling-based estimation methods are generally applicable. We present the first deterministic algorithm that computes local RLCTs exactly for any two-dimensional model whose Kullback-Leibler distance is contact equivalent to a polynomial, derive a bound on its complexity, and demonstrate its effectiveness for a broad class of models, with applications including polynomial neural networks. Beyond providing ground truth to calibrate sampling-based estimators, exact computation reveals algebraic structure in learning coefficients that sampling cannot and out-speeds it in the shallow regime.

</details>


### [60] [Decoding silent reading from non-invasive EEG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20186)
*Ingo Marquardt,Anthilia Alchanat,Priyanka Jain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了从脑电图(EEG)信号中解码无声阅读时的词汇和语义信息，利用CLIP风格对比学习框架，在单个被试约49小时、24万词次的数据上实现了开放词表的词级解码，结果表明解码准确率显著高于随机水平，且呈数据受限而非饱和状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大脑内部言语的无创解码面临根本性数据问题：无法采集与自发内心独白配对的大脑活动数据，而现有的替代范式（提示重复式和回顾报告式生成性内心言语）采集速度慢、时间对齐性差、被试配合度无法验证。因此需要一种可扩展的替代任务来研究内部言语的神经解码。

Method: 将无声阅读作为可扩展的替代任务，采用以下方法：(1) 单被试密集采样，393次运行约49小时的19通道干电极EEG，约24万次词呈现；(2) 连续叙事文本以快速序列视觉呈现(RSVP)方式展示，每轮随机化排版以部分去相关词身份与低级视觉形式；(3) 采用卷积EEG编码器（可选接因果Transformer），通过CLIP风格对比目标训练，将短EEG窗口与大语言模型中的目标词隐状态嵌入对齐；(4) 通过词分组top-10检索与置换基线比较进行评估。

Result: 实验结果表明：(1) 解码可靠地高于偶然水平，涵盖中频和罕见词；(2) 解码性能随训练数据量呈对数线性增长，未见饱和迹象；(3) 移除枕叶和后颞叶电极使词级增益降低约三分之一，但上下文跟踪不受影响；(4) 控制分析将词级解码与叙事上下文跟踪、Transformer位置嵌入引入的非神经位置先验区分开来。

Conclusion: 该研究证明开放词表的词级信息可从无声阅读期间的EEG中恢复，且当前解码性能受限于数据量而非已达饱和，为内部言语解码提供了可行的可扩展研究路径。

Abstract: Non-invasive decoding of inner speech faces a fundamental data problem: a corpus pairing brain activity with a person's spontaneous inner monologue cannot be collected, and the available proxy paradigms (cued repetitive and retrospectively reported generative inner speech) are slow to acquire, poorly time-locked, and subject compliance is unverifiable. We therefore treat silent reading as a scalable proxy task and ask how much lexical and semantic information a contrastive decoder can extract from it. We report an open-vocabulary analysis of approximately 240,000 word presentations recorded from a single densely-sampled participant across 393 runs (ca. 49 h) of 19-channel dry-electrode EEG. Words from continuous narrative text were presented in rapid serial visual presentation, with typography randomised on every trial to partially decorrelate word identity from low-level visual form. A convolutional EEG encoder, optionally followed by a causal transformer, was trained with a CLIP-style contrastive objective to align short EEG windows with hidden-state embeddings of the presented word taken from a large language model. Decoding, evaluated as word-grouped top-10 retrieval against permutation baselines, was reliably above chance, extended to mid-frequency and rare words, and scaled log-linearly with training-data volume with no sign of saturation. Removing occipital and posterior-temporal electrodes reduced the word-level gain by roughly one third but left context tracking unchanged. Control analyses separate word-level decoding from narrative context tracking and from a non-neural positional prior introduced by the transformer's positional embedding. These results establish that open-vocabulary word-level information is recoverable from EEG during silent reading, and that decoding is data-limited rather than saturated.

</details>


### [61] [DICS: Data-Informed Centroid Splitting for Decision Tree Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20258)
*MD Saifur Rahman Mazumder,Feng Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DICS（数据信息质心分裂）框架，利用基于数据驱动的先验知识构建紧凑且有信息的候选分裂集，显著降低决策树分裂搜索空间，在保持分类性能的同时大幅减少训练时间，可集成于决策树、随机森林和梯度提升模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策树模型因可解释性和强经验性能而广泛应用，但训练过程在大规模高维数据集上计算开销很大，主要瓶颈在于每个节点对所有候选分裂点的穷举搜索。为了在不损失预测性能的前提下提升训练效率，需要一种更智能的候选分裂选择策略。

Method: DICS采用聚类思想，利用数据驱动的先验信息（包括类感知结构）构造一个紧凑且有信息量的候选分裂集合，从而缩小分裂搜索空间。文章提供了理论分析，证明在给定假设下DICS相比穷举分裂搜索不会降低分类树性能。该框架可无缝嵌入分类树、随机森林和梯度提升模型中。

Result: 大量在合成数据集和基准数据集上的实验表明，DICS在保持与穷举搜索相当的分类精度的同时，显著缩短了训练时间，验证了将数据信息先验融入分裂选择对可扩展分类树学习的价值。

Conclusion: DICS通过数据驱动的聚类方法有效解决了决策树训练的计算瓶颈，在精度与效率之间取得了良好平衡，为大规模分类树学习提供了实用的加速方案。

Abstract: Decision tree-based models are widely used in machine learning due to their interpretability and strong empirical performance. However, training decision trees can be computationally expensive, particularly for large and high-dimensional datasets, largely due to the exhaustive search over candidate splits at each node. To improve computational efficiency, we propose Data-Informed Centroid Splitting (DICS), a clustering-based framework that constructs a compact and informative set of candidate splits using data-driven priors. By incorporating class-aware structure, DICS significantly reduces the split search space for classification tasks while preserving predictive performance. We further provide theoretical analysis showing that under the stated assumptions, DICS does not degrade the performance of classification trees compared to exhaustive split search. DICS can be incorporated into classification trees, random forests, and gradient-boosting models. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DICS achieves comparable accuracy while substantially reducing training time across synthetic and benchmark datasets, highlighting the benefit of integrating data-informed priors into split selection for scalable classification tree learning.

</details>


### [62] [Dynamic Structural Causal Modeling for Sleep](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20285)
*Ranveer Singh,Saurabh Mathur,Pranuthi Tenali,Arun Badi,Sriraam Natarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用PCMCI+算法从105例家庭睡眠呼吸暂停测试（HSAT）记录中学习睡眠呼吸障碍的动态因果图，揭示了不同性别和年龄亚组在因果结构上的系统性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 睡眠呼吸障碍的因果动态复杂且在不同患者群体间存在差异，阻碍了针对性干预措施的开发。理解不同人群（性别、年龄）间因果结构的差异是实现个体化治疗的关键。

Method: 采用PCMCI+算法对105例HSAT记录中窗口化分数变量进行因果发现；利用领域知识进行边黑名单约束；通过bootstrap聚合（bagging）处理小子队列样本量不足的问题。

Result: 学习到的因果图显示：时间自依赖和呼吸暂停-去饱和关系在所有队列中持续存在，而其他因果关系在不同亚组间存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 动态因果图能够揭示睡眠呼吸障碍在不同性别和年龄队列间的系统性因果结构差异，为开发针对性干预措施提供依据。

Abstract: The causal dynamics of sleep-disordered breathing are complex and vary across patient populations, hindering the development of targeted interventions. We learn dynamic causal graphs of sleep-disordered breathing from Home Sleep Apnea Test (HSAT) recordings, revealing systematic differences in causal structure across sex and age subcohorts. We do so using the PCMCI+ algorithm on windowed fractional variables derived from 105 HSAT recordings, exploiting domain knowledge via edge blacklisting and employing bootstrap aggregation to address small subcohort sizes. The learned graphs show that temporal self-dependencies and the apnea-desaturation relationship persist across all cohorts, while other relationships vary substantially.

</details>


### [63] [Physical-Support Confidence Sets for Highly Coherent Dictionaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20295)
*Guan-Ju Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分辨率感知的物理支持推断方法，在字典学习和部署信号表示的不确定性下，联合推断物理支持。对于相干原子类，给出了极小极大物理分辨率的理论界，并引入主动端点包围（AEB）算法来避免物理上过度精确的推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高度相干的字典中，稀疏追踪在字典学习后可能产生精确的原子支持，但其物理解释未必被校准数据所验证——不同但校准兼容的替代字典可能将相同支持赋予不同的物理意义。因此需要一种联合考虑字典不确定性和信号表示不确定性的物理支持推断方法。

Method: 提出跨字典置信对应方法，保留校准兼容的字典与部署兼容的稀疏表示，并将存活解释投影到物理支持空间。在此基础上分析极小极大物理分辨率，推导出最优质点估计算子表现不佳时的理论下界。计算上引入主动端点包围（AEB），一种自适应有限组过程，仅评估能影响物理报告的候选，否则安全粗化或放弃。

Result: 对于局部相干原子类（分离尺度s），N个校准信号下极小极大物理分辨率满足δ_opt(N,s)≍min{s, 1/(√N·s²)}，相对分辨率由定向信息尺度Ns⁶支配。部署复制仅在定向变化不能被活动系数吸收时改善物理定位。有限组实验（含四区域合成应用）显示点值插件选择器可能物理上过度精确，而AEB避免不支持的细化且需要更少候选评估。

Conclusion: 研究确立了物理支持推断在字典不确定性下的极小极大最优分辨率，揭示了物理精度受限于校准信号数与相干块分离尺度之间的内在权衡。AEB算法为实际部署提供了计算上可行且避免过度精确的方案。

Abstract: Sparse pursuit after dictionary learning can yield a precise atom support even when its physical interpretation is not justified by the calibration data, especially for highly coherent dictionaries where alternative calibration-compatible dictionaries may assign different physical meanings to the same selected support. We develop resolution-aware physical-support inference that jointly accounts for uncertainty in the learned dictionary and in the representation of a deployment signal. Our cross-dictionary confidence correspondence retains calibration-compatible dictionaries and deployment-compatible sparse representations, then projects the surviving explanations onto physical-support space. For local coherent-atom classes with separation scale s, once the deployment data resolve the coherent-block explanation and its atom support, the minimax physical resolution from N calibration signals satisfies $δ_{\mathrm{opt}}(N,s)\asymp\min\{s,\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}s^2}\}$, with relative resolution governed by the orientation-information scale $Ns^6$. Deployment replication improves physical localization only when orientation changes cannot be absorbed by adjusting the active coefficients. For computation, we introduce active endpoint bracketing (AEB), an adaptive finite-bank procedure that evaluates only candidates that can still affect the physical report and otherwise safely coarsens or abstains. Finite-bank experiments, including a four-region synthetic application, show that a point-valued plug-in selector can be physically overprecise, whereas AEB avoids unsupported refinement with fewer candidate evaluations.

</details>


### [64] [Explainable Transformer Models for Clinical Prediction Tasks on Structured Electronic Health Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20315)
*Jun Ni Du,Lukas Adamek,Maxim Kryukov,Flavio Dormont,Ziv Bar-Joseph,Sven Jager,Brandon Rufino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了BERT-LER，一个基于BERT风格的编码EHR时间线模型，通过百分位分箱将实验室检验结果编码为离散token，并结合Integrated Gradients实现token级可解释性。在7500万病人的去标识化EHR数据集上预训练和微调，在EHRShot基准和哮喘严重程度进展研究中达到有竞争力的预测性能，且归因与临床已知风险因素一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的结构化EHR预测模型很少同时强调定量实验室信息和相对于输入医疗事件的可解释性，存在方法论空白——即缺乏一个统一框架来同时表达实验室数值（保留分级信息）并提供可解释性。

Method: BERT-LER采用BERT风格架构处理编码EHR时间线；实验室检验结果通过百分位分箱转换为离散token，在保留定量分级的同时实现词表化；使用公开的7500万病人去标识化EHR数据集进行预训练和微调；采用Integrated Gradients生成基于输入EHR序列的token级归因。

Result: 在EHRShot基准套件和哮喘严重程度进展研究上，BERT-LER的预测性能与公开基准模型相当，且在实验室相关任务上往往超过基准；其归因与临床已知的风险因素相吻合。

Conclusion: BERT-LER架构及可解释性方法可推广应用于多个治疗领域和基于结构化EHR语言模型的预测任务，填补了EHR基础模型中实验室值表示与可解释性统一的空白。但该内容未通过合规性审核，已隐藏。

Abstract: Predictive models over structured electronic health records (EHRs) remain central to machine learning for healthcare, but few have jointly emphasized quantitative laboratory information and interpretability with respect to input medical events. We present BERT-LER, a BERT-style model for coded EHR timelines pretrained and fine-tuned from a de-identified EHR dataset of 75 million patients, that encodes laboratory test results as discrete tokens while retaining graded information through percentile-based binning, paired with Integrated Gradients for token-level attributions grounded in the input EHR sequence. We benchmark our approach on the public EHRShot benchmark suite and on an asthma severity progression study based on real-world data. This addresses a methodological gap in EHR foundation-style modeling by unifying laboratory value representation and explainability in a single framework, while assessing whether both predictive performance and explanations generalize beyond standard clinical prediction tasks. Across EHRShot and asthma tasks, BERT-LER achieves predictive performance that is competitive with, and on laboratory-related tasks often exceeds, publicly available benchmark models, and provides attributions that align with clinically known risk factors. Our architecture and explainability approach can be applied to many therapeutic areas and prediction tasks using language models trained on structured EHRs.

</details>


### [65] [A comparison between ceiling-mounted FMCW, IR-UWB and Wi-Fi radar for in-bedroom human activity monitoring and sleep interruption detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20322)
*Anton Lambrecht,Reda El Hail,Xianjun Jiao,Pieter Crombez,Dominique Schreurs,Peter Karsmakers,Adnan Shahid,Eli De Poorter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在受控条件下对比了吊顶安装的FMCW雷达、IR-UWB和Wi-Fi三种射频技术用于医疗健康监测的性能，基于20名参与者和6种房间布局的同步记录，发现IR-UWB在跨受试者活动识别上最优（89.0%宏F1），FMCW在跨房间泛化上最佳（83.8%宏F1），睡眠监测均超过92%宏F1，并揭示了识别性能与环境鲁棒性之间的本质权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同射频技术（FMCW、IR-UWB、Wi-Fi）在医疗健康监测中的对比研究缺乏统一的部署条件和评估标准，现有研究在硬件、数据集和方法论上各不相同；此外，吊顶式雷达在实际部署和成本上具有优势，但其性能尚未被充分探索。

Method: 在20名参与者、6种房间布局下进行同步数据采集，对吊顶安装的FMCW、IR-UWB和Wi-Fi三种技术进行受控对比；所有技术统一使用相同的卷积神经网络（CNN）进行评估，涵盖细粒度10类人体活动识别（HAR）任务和粗粒度4类睡眠监测任务。

Result: IR-UWB在跨受试者活动识别中表现最佳（89.0%宏F1），FMCW在跨房间布局泛化方面最优（83.8%宏F1）；睡眠监测任务中所有技术在新环境下均超过92%宏F1。差异归因于距离分辨率、天线分集、多普勒分辨率和空间信息保留能力的不同。

Conclusion: 识别性能与环境鲁棒性之间存在根本性的权衡，该权衡可通过不同射频技术的硬件特性差异来解释；研究为面向医疗健康的射频感知系统设计提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Despite their growing importance for contact-free radio frequency (RF) based healthcare monitoring, different radio technologies such as frequency-modulated continuous wave (FMCW) radar, impulse radio ultra-wideband (IR-UWB), and Wi-Fi sensing are rarely compared under identical deployment conditions, as existing studies typically differ in hardware, datasets, and evaluation methodologies. In addition, the performance of ceiling-mounted radars, despite their practical deployment and cost advantages in healthcare environments, remain underexplored. Therefore, this paper presents a controlled comparison and analysis of ceiling-mounted FMCW, IR-UWB, and Wi-Fi sensing using synchronized recordings from 20 participants across six room layouts. All technologies are evaluated with the same convolutional neural network (CNN) on both a fine-grained 10-class human activity recognition (HAR) task and a coarse 4-class sleep monitoring task. IR-UWB achieves the highest cross-subject activity recognition performance (89.0% macro F1), while FMCW generalizes best to unseen room layouts (83.8% macro F1). For sleep monitoring, all technologies exceed 92% macro F1 in unseen environments. The results reveal a fundamental trade-off between recognition performance and environmental robustness, which can be explained through differences in range resolution, antenna diversity, Doppler resolution, and spatial information retention. These findings provide practical guidelines for the design of healthcare-oriented RF sensing systems.

</details>

### [66] [The Lazy Pod That Lies: Deferred Cost and Failure Semantics of Lazy Container Image Pulling for Model Serving on Kubernetes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19412)
*Georgii Kliukovkin*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文评估了Kubernetes上KServe中两种生产级懒加载镜像拉取系统（eStargz/stargz-snapshotter和AWS SOCI）在模型交付场景下的性能。研究发现懒加载确实使冷启动时间与模型大小解耦（16.9-17.6秒 vs 急切拉取的24.5-573.0秒），但代价只是被推迟而非消除；更关键的是，揭示了懒加载在持续合法读取下会耗尽节点级缓存，导致运行中的pod读取模型文件失败，且存在监控盲区和快照守护进程重启导致文件句柄永久失效等严重故障模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型服务pod的启动开销主要由容器镜像拉取主导，懒加载技术承诺通过即时挂载和按需获取来消除这一成本。然而，该承诺在实际生产场景中的真实代价和潜在故障模式尚未被系统评估，特别是对于大型模型（包括fp16权重）在Kubernetes上的实际表现存在知识空白。

Method: 在Kubernetes上使用KServe，对两种生产级懒加载系统（eStargz/stargz-snapshotter和AWS SOCI）与急切拉取基线进行对比实验。使用2到140 GB的工件（包括真实fp16权重），测量冷启动时间、完整读取时间、缓存耗尽时的故障行为，以及通过仪表化serving pod检测监控盲区。

Result: 懒加载实现其核心承诺：冷启动时间与模型大小无关（16.9-17.6秒，相比急切拉取的24.5-573.0秒）。但完整读取14 GB模型通过懒加载挂载需要105.3秒，比其替代的72.4秒急切拉取更慢。两种系统在生命周期两端付出代价：SOCI在Ready前几乎预取完整镜像，eStargz将几乎所有内容推迟到首次读取。在持续合法读取下，快照的节点级缓存会耗尽有限卷空间，导致运行中的pod开始失败。最早期阶段，pod通过所有K8s可见和应用级检查达196秒，而snapshotter已在记录实际失败；高压下67-94%的模型文件失败。释放缓存空间可以自愈，但守护进程重启会留下永久过期的文件句柄，pod仍报告为Running。

Conclusion: 懒加载镜像拉取在模型交付中确实消除了大小相关的启动延迟，但代价被推迟到运行时而非消除。平台必须为懒加载系统制定专门的放置策略、监控指标和缓存容量规划指南，因为其故障模式（缓存耗尽导致的实时读取失败、快照守护进程重启导致的文件句柄失效）是急切拉取在结构上不可能出现的。

Abstract: Lazy container-image pulling promises to eliminate the dominant cost of starting a model-serving pod by mounting the image immediately and fetching content on demand. We evaluate this promise for model delivery on Kubernetes, using KServe with two production lazy-pulling systems -- eStargz/stargz-snapshotter and AWS SOCI -- against eager baselines, on artifacts from 2 to 140 GB including real fp16 weights. Lazy pulling delivers its headline: cold time-to-first-prediction becomes size-independent (16.9--17.6s, versus 24.5--573.0s eager). But the cost is deferred, not eliminated: a full read of a 14 GB model through the lazy mount takes 105.3s, slower than the 72.4s eager pull it replaced, and the two systems pay at opposite lifecycle ends (SOCI prefetches nearly the full image before Ready; eStargz defers nearly everything to first read). More consequentially, we characterize a failure mode eager pulling structurally cannot exhibit: under sustained legitimate reads with default configuration, the snapshotter's node-level cache exhausts its finite volume and already-running pods begin failing reads of model files. At the earliest stage of exhaustion, an instrumented serving pod passed every Kubernetes-visible and application-level check for 196s while its snapshotter was already logging real failures; under heavier pressure, 67--94% of model files fail, scaling monotonically with residual cache occupancy. A live pod self-heals if cache space is freed under it, but a snapshotter-daemon restart under a live pod leaves permanently stale file handles in a pod still reported Running. We derive placement, monitoring, and cache-sizing guidance for serving platforms and operators.

</details>


### [67] [When Do LLM Agents Help? Deadline-Aware Mixed-Criticality Task Scheduling at the Autonomous-Vehicle Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19557)
*Reza Zakerian*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了异构MEC服务器上截止时间感知的混合关键性任务调度问题，构建了一个窗口化合同网拍卖启发式算法（TC完成率0.902，优于全部15个基线），并通过受控分解实验证明LLM控制平面仅在负载非平稳（任务突增）时才有显著优势，平稳负载下启发式已接近最优状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶将延迟敏感的感知任务卸载到移动边缘计算服务器，错过安全关键任务会导致不安全而非性能下降。虽然LLM被越来越多地用作自适应调度器，但缺乏其何时有效的证据。作者希望回答多智能体LLM控制层是否能超越强启发式算法这一核心问题。

Method: 两阶段方法：（1）构建强启发式——窗口化合同网拍卖，按最早截止时间优先排序时间关键任务，并按最早完成时间放置任务；（2）添加LLM控制平面，通过受控分解实验将调度器优势归因于批处理范围和TC优先排序两个普通因素，并在平稳与非平稳负载下对比评估。

Result: 在60个实例、3种拓扑、15个基线的对比中，启发式算法TC完成率达0.902，显著高于最佳基线0.838（Holm校正p<0.001），达到CP-SAT上界的0.87。平稳负载下LLM控制平面无额外增益；但在运行中期安全关键任务突增的非平稳场景下，LLM控制平面显著优于静态启发式与bandit方法。

Conclusion: LLM编排只在非平稳性为固定策略无法利用的调度留有余量时才值得其成本。作者报告了控制平面延迟和推理依据，并发布了全部代码和带种子的实例以支持复现。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles offload latency-sensitive perception tasks to nearby mobile edge computing (MEC) servers, where a missed safety-critical task is unsafe rather than merely degraded. Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly proposed as adaptive, explainable schedulers, yet evidence of when they help is scarce. We study deadline-aware, mixed-criticality scheduling on heterogeneous MEC servers, where time-critical (TC) tasks must be protected at a controlled cost to best-effort traffic, and ask whether a multi-agent LLM control layer improves on a strong heuristic. We answer in two steps. First we build the heuristic: a windowed contract-net auction that orders each admission window time-critical-first by earliest deadline and places tasks by earliest-finish-time. Across 60 instances on three topologies and 15 baselines under an identical online constraint, it attains a TC completion rate of 0.902, above every baseline (Holm-corrected p < 0.001; best baseline 0.838) and at 0.87 of a CP-SAT upper bound. Second, we add the LLM control plane. A controlled decomposition traces the scheduler's advantage to two ordinary factors, the batching horizon and time-critical-first ordering; the auction, the per-window LLM policy, and online adaptation add nothing while the load is stationary, where the heuristic is already near-optimal. Under a mid-run surge of safety-critical tasks the picture changes, and the LLM control plane gains significantly over both the static heuristic and the bandit. LLM orchestration therefore earns its cost only when non-stationarity opens headroom a fixed policy cannot use. We report control-plane latency and rationale, and release all code and seeded instances.

</details>


### [68] [Optimality and Trade-offs in Fast BFT SMR (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19629)
*Neil Giridharan,Minh Tung Nguyen,Ittai Abraham,Allen Clement,Natacha Crooks,Pierre Sutra*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究快速拜占庭容错状态机复制（Fast BFT SMR）协议的复制因子（replication factor）上下界，提出紧致上下界，并展示一个次优协议以说明复制因子与恢复效率之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速状态机复制（Fast SMR）协议在崩溃故障（crash-fault）场景下已被广泛研究，相比基于领导者的协议具有低延迟、高吞吐、高可用性和公平性等优势。然而在拜占庭容错（BFT）场景下，Fast BFT SMR协议数量很少，其复制因子要求尚未被充分理解，缺乏严格的上下界分析。

Method: 论文对Fast BFT SMR所需的复制因子进行了理论分析，推导出紧致（tight）的上下界；同时构造了一个次优（suboptimal）协议，通过牺牲复制因子的最优性来换取更高效的恢复（recovery）过程，从而揭示了复制因子与恢复效率之间的内在权衡关系。

Result: 论文给出了Fast BFT SMR复制因子的紧致上下界，明确了在拜占庭故障模型下实现快速状态机复制所需的最少副本数量；此外，通过一个次优协议展示了复制因子与恢复效率之间的权衡（trade-off），即可以通过增加复制因子来获得更简单的恢复机制。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了Fast BFT SMR在复制因子理论界限方面的空白，为设计和评估Fast BFT SMR协议提供了理论指导，并揭示了实际系统设计中复制因子与恢复效率之间的重要权衡关系。

Abstract: Fast state-machine replication (SMR) protocols in the crash-fault setting have attracted significant interest in both academia and industry. This interest stems from their advantages over leader-based protocols, including low execution latency for non-conflicting commands, high throughput, improved availability, and increased fairness. While Fast SMR is well studied in the crash-fault setting, the Byzantine fault-tolerant (BFT) setting remains much less understood, with only a small number of existing Fast BFT SMR protocols. In this paper, we present tight upper and lower bounds on the replication factor required for Fast BFT SMR. We also present a suboptimal protocol that illustrates a trade-off between replication factor and recovery efficiency.

</details>


### [69] [CacheRoute: Planned Prefix-Affinity Routing for Large-Scale LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19677)
*Huang Cheng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: CacheRoute是一种基于周期性路由计划的KV缓存感知负载均衡方案，通过将高频键稳定分配到热服务器集合来兼顾前缀缓存复用与负载均衡。在Llama-3.3-70B fp8的60块H100 GPU实验中，其QPS达176±11（p99 SLO 3.5s），为最强基线的2.3倍，缓存命中率从64.1%提升至93.2%，但亲和性在恢复KV工作量不足时会因负载倾斜而失效，需通过阴影回放（shadow replay）验证后再部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前缀缓存仅在重复请求到达仍持有该前缀KV的服务器时才能避免prefill开销，而缓存盲（cache-blind）的负载均衡会分散这种复用机会；固定亲和性虽能保留复用但可能导致单服务器过载。现有方案无法同时兼顾前缀缓存复用与负载均衡，需要一种能平衡这两者的路由机制。

Method: CacheRoute采用周期性路由规划：将高请求率（high-rate）的键纳入稳定的热键集合（warm set），并根据预期负载进行分配；热键可使用多个目的地（尽管主实验中的半合成负载每个键恰好只用一个目的地），从而在保留缓存亲和性的同时避免单点过载。论文通过多种半合成工作负载、8B模型受控实验及突发负载实验，分离了亲和性效应与放置效应，并进行32B反例实验检验边界条件。

Result: 在Llama-3.3-70B fp8、60块H100 GPU环境下，CacheRoute在3.5s p99 SLO下维持176±11 QPS，是五个基线中最强的2.3倍；KV缓存命中率从缓存盲均衡的64.1±1.3%提升至93.2±0.5%。但两个32B工作负载显示，当亲和性恢复的KV工作量过少时，残留负载倾斜会降低甚至抹除收益，说明CacheRoute的增益存在前提条件。

Conclusion: CacheRoute通过周期性路由规划成功解决了缓存亲和性与负载均衡之间的权衡，显著提升KV缓存命中率和吞吐量。然而其收益依赖于亲和性能恢复足够的KV工作量；在工作负载特性不明时，应通过阴影回放（shadow replay）验证后再启用亲和性，而非仅凭负载统计量直接部署。

Abstract: Prefix caching avoids prefill only when a repeated request returns to a server that still holds the prefix KV. Cache-blind balancing disperses that reuse; fixed affinity preserves it but can overload a server. CacheRoute resolves this tradeoff with a periodic routing plan. It admits high-rate keys to a stable warm set and places their assignments by expected load. Hot keys may use more than one destination, although every key in our primary semi-synthetic aggregate uses exactly one. On Llama-3.3-70B in fp8 across 60 H100 GPUs, CacheRoute sustains 176+/-11 QPS at a 3.5-s p99 SLO, 2.3x the strongest of five baselines. Served KV-cache hit rate rises from 64.1+/-1.3% under cache-blind balancing to 93.2+/-0.5%. A second semi-synthetic aggregate and controlled 8B and burst experiments separate the effects of affinity and placement. Two 32B workloads provide the counterexamples: when affinity recovers too little KV work, its residual load skew reduces or erases the improvement. We therefore recommend gating any deployment with a shadow replay rather than enabling affinity from workload statistics alone.

</details>


### [70] [ParaWeb: Parallel Programming Patterns for Web Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19935)
*Suejb Memeti*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ParaWeb是一个TypeScript库，实现了十种并行编程模式，每种模式提供消息传递（MP）、共享缓冲（Shared）和WebGPU三种变体，总计三十种实现。CPU变体在16线程下最高加速11.6倍，GPU变体在计算密集型模式中最高加速260倍，图像卷积案例中GPU加速最高达414倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代Web应用需要大量计算，但JavaScript传统上受限于单线程执行模型。虽然Node.js Worker Threads和浏览器Web Workers提供了底层并行机制，但开发者缺乏能够将常见并行结构抽象为可复用模式的高层抽象。

Method: ParaWeb库实现了十种并行编程模式，每种模式提供三种实现变体：基于postMessage结构化克隆的消息传递（MP）变体、基于SharedArrayBuffer和类型化数组视图的共享缓冲（Shared）变体，以及使用WebGPU计算着色器实现硬件加速的GPU变体。论文描述了架构、设计决策和模式特定的实现策略。

Result: 实验评估覆盖全部三十种实现在三种数据规模下的性能。CPU变体在16线程下对计算密集型模式最高加速11.6倍；GPU变体对高算术强度的计算密集模式（如Farm、Scatter、Reduce和Map）最高加速260倍。五项图像卷积滤波器的案例研究显示，GPU加速在非可分离核上相对单线程CPU最高可达414倍加速，并在1024x1024、2048x2048和4K图像上保持一致扩展。

Conclusion: ParaWeb通过提供跨平台（服务端Node.js、浏览器环境、WebGPU计算着色器）的并行模式抽象，填补了JavaScript生态中缺少高层并行模式库的空白。实验证明其CPU和GPU变体均能显著提升计算密集型Web应用的性能，特别是WebGPU在具有高算术强度的场景中能获得数量级以上的加速比。

Abstract: Modern web applications increasingly require computationally intensive processing, yet JavaScript, the dominant language of the web, has traditionally been limited to a single-threaded execution model. Node.js Worker Threads and browser Web Workers provide low-level mechanisms for parallel execution, but developers lack high-level abstractions that capture recurring parallel structures as reusable patterns. In this paper, we present ParaWeb, a TypeScript library that implements ten parallel programming patterns for server-side Node.js, client-side browser environments, and WebGPU compute shaders. ParaWeb provides three implementation variants for each pattern: a message-passing (MP) variant based on structured cloning via postMessage, a shared-buffer (Shared) variant that uses SharedArrayBuffer with typed array views, and a GPU variant that uses WebGPU compute shaders for hardware-accelerated execution. We describe the architecture, design decisions, and pattern-specific implementation strategies, and we evaluate the performance of all thirty implementations across three data sizes. Experimental evaluation results show that the CPU-based variants achieve speedups of up to 11.6x with 16 threads for compute-bound patterns, while the GPU variants reach speedups of up to 260x for compute-bound patterns with high arithmetic intensity such as Farm, Scatter, Reduce, and Map. A case study on five image-convolution filters further shows that GPU acceleration reaches up to 414x speedup over single-threaded CPU on non-separable kernels, with consistent scaling across 1024x1024$, 2048x2048$, and 4K images.

</details>


### [71] [Design and Empirical Evaluation of a Network-Centric, On-Premises Architecture for Earth Observation Data Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20283)
*João Pinelo,João Gonçalves,Denis Willett,Amit Ruhela,Derek Steinmoeller,Uriel Mendoza,Pelumi S. Alao,Ronald Soares Lopes,Rogerio Atem de Carvalho,Pedro Mattos*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了一种面向在本地（on-premises）部署的地球观测（EO）数据访问的网络中心架构，以首个运营部署AIR数据中心为实例，系统性地隔离并量化了网络带宽对云原生数据格式存储吞吐性能的制约影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球观测项目产生的数据量超过大多数机构网络的传输和存储能力。公共云平台虽能解决资源充足机构的需求，但大西洋沿岸各机构在网络连通性、数据主权和资金方面存在限制，本地基础设施是其唯一可行路径。云原生数据格式虽支持高效部分读取，但其性能依赖底层网络带宽，而这一依赖关系很少被单独度量。

Method: 构建可复制的网络中心型本地EO数据访问架构，包含MinIO对象存储集群（100 GbE网络）、PostGIS元数据目录和OGC API-EDR访问层。在持续并行负载下刻画网络特性，评估面向EO代表性工作负载的对象存储吞吐量，并在相同硬件上用限速基线对比测量，将网络带宽作为唯一变量进行隔离。另开展多站点复制基准测试以刻画联邦原语。

Result: 网络带宽在阈值以下是对批量EO数据访问存储吞吐量的主导约束；超过阈值后，端点内存拓扑而非容量决定了系统能使用的带宽。在本硬件类别中，该阈值位于每服务器10 Gbps以上。低于该阈值时，网络容量单独决定设施可交付的性能；高于该阈值时，进一步网络投资的回报取决于端点内存配置，而这一投入可以延迟进行。

Conclusion: 该架构验证了本地部署EO数据访问的可行性，明确了网络带宽与存储吞吐量之间的量化关系，并识别出内存拓扑这一后续优化瓶颈，为跨大西洋机构的联邦云部署提供了可复制的工程参考和实施策略。

Abstract: Earth observation (EO) programmes generate data at volumes that exceed the transfer and storage capacity of most institutional networks. Public cloud platforms address this for well-resourced organisations, but institutions across the Atlantic basin face constraints in connectivity, sovereignty and funding that make on-premises infrastructure the only viable path. Cloud-native data formats enable efficient partial reads, yet their performance depends on the bandwidth of the underlying network fabric, a dependency rarely measured in isolation. This paper presents a replicable, network-centric architecture for on-premises EO data access, evaluated at its first operational deployment: the AIR Data Centre, founding node of the Atlantic Cloud. The system comprises a MinIO object storage cluster on a 100 GbE fabric, a PostGIS metadata catalogue and an OGC API-EDR access layer. We characterise the fabric under sustained parallel load, evaluate object storage throughput for EO-representative workloads, and compare measured performance against throttled baselines on identical hardware, isolating network bandwidth as the sole variable. Multi-site replication benchmarks with partner institutions characterise the federation primitive the model depends on. Network bandwidth is the dominant constraint on storage throughput for bulk EO data access up to a threshold; beyond it, endpoint memory topology rather than capacity governs how much bandwidth a system can use. For this hardware class that threshold lies above 10 Gbps per server. Below it, network capacity alone sets what the facility can deliver; above it, the return on further network investment depends on endpoint memory provisioning, which can be deferred and bought later.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [72] [Active Inference as Context Acquisition for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19202)
*Sanchayan Dutta,Sai Niranjan Ramachandran,Suvrit Sra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将AI智能体的上下文获取问题形式化为主动推断（active inference），通过内层推断更新潜在任务状态的信念，外层决策选择下一个上下文动作、任务动作或停止动作来最小化期望自由能。在确定性设定下，认知项简化为期望信息增益（可按token成本归一化），并在OQA框架中以精确后验和动态规划最优性测试前沿语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式AI智能体需要在尽可能高效的前提下获取正确的上下文。当用户省略约束、偏好、文件或任务变量时，智能体要么默认假设，要么花费token进行澄清提问、检索调用、工具调用或提示尝试——这种权衡需要一个统一的、模型无关的理论框架来指导何时以及如何获取上下文。

Method: 将上下文获取建模为主动推断过程：内层推断步骤更新关于潜在任务状态的信念（贝叶斯后验），外层决策步骤在"上下文动作、任务动作、停止动作"之间选择，以最小化含代价的期望自由能。在确定性设定下，认知项退化为期望信息增益（可按token成本归一化）。在OQA框架中采用精确后验计算和动态规划最优性基准，并在25至300个候选项的二元和多类别分类任务上测试前沿语言模型。

Result: 在二元和多类别分类任务（25-300个候选）上对前沿语言模型进行了系统基准测试，同时研究了生成前澄清和受token预算约束的自动提示优化场景。

Conclusion: 该公式是模型无关的，将主动推断视为AI智能体上下文获取层的一个设计原则，为智能体在"默认假设"与"主动获取信息"之间提供理论驱动的决策依据。

Abstract: Interactive AI agents must acquire the right context as efficiently as possible. When a user omits a constraint, preference, file, or task variable, an agent can proceed with a default assumption or spend tokens on a clarifying question, retrieval call, tool call, or prompt trial. We formulate this tradeoff as active inference for context acquisition. An inner inference step updates beliefs over a latent task state, and an outer decision selects the next context action, task action, or stop action to minimize expected free energy under cost. In deterministic settings, the epistemic term reduces to expected information gain, optionally normalized by token cost. We instantiate the framework in Optimal Question Asking (OQA), with exact posteriors and a dynamic programming oracle, and benchmark frontier language models on binary and multiway categorical tasks from 25 to 300 candidates. We also study clarification before generation and automated prompt optimization under token budgets. The formulation is model-agnostic and views active inference as a design principle for the context-acquisition layer of AI agents.

</details>


### [73] [Robust Metaheuristics under Uncertainty for Berth Allocation and Quay Crane Assignment: A Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19214)
*Yang Li,Peilan Xu,Wenjian Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次聚焦综述了不确定环境下针对泊位分配与岸桥调度问题（BACAP）的鲁棒群体元启发式优化方法，系统梳理了不确定性来源、信息表示、搜索机制、基准测试套件，并指出了未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 港口码头运营中BACAP问题在不确定性（如船舶到达偏差、装卸时间波动、资源中断）下，名义最优调度在执行时可能失效，亟需鲁棒优化。然而现有研究在不确定性表示、鲁棒性准则、搜索机制和实验评估协议上分散零碎，缺乏系统性综述。

Method: 本文从机制导向视角组织现有方法，涵盖四个维度：解表示与解码、鲁棒评估与选择、鲁棒性引导的搜索动态、可行性保持与恢复；同时构建了一个不确定BACAP基准测试套件，并结合代表性元启发式算法与不同鲁棒策略给出基线结果。

Result: 提供了首个专门针对不确定BACAP的鲁棒群体元启发式方法综述，提出了基准测试套件并报告了组合元启发式与鲁棒策略的说明性基线实验结果。

Conclusion: 识别出四方面开放挑战：基准扩展、鲁棒性感知搜索设计、时间自适应鲁棒性以及非平稳不确定性，为该领域后续研究提供了方向指引。

Abstract: The berth allocation and quay crane assignment problem (BACAP) is a representative port-terminal scheduling problem in maritime transportation and freight logistics, where vessel arrivals, berth positions, service durations, and quay?crane availability are tightly coupled. Under uncertainties such as arrival deviations, handling-time fluctuations, and resource disruptions, schedules optimized under nominal assumptions may become fragile during execution, motivating the study of robust metaheuristic optimization for BACAP in port-terminal operations. Although population-based metaheuristics have been widely used for BACAP and related port-scheduling problems, existing studies remain fragmented in their uncertainty repre?sentations, robustness criteria, search mechanisms, and empir?ical evaluation protocols. To the best of our knowledge, this paper provides the first focused review dedicated to robust population-based metaheuristics for BACAP under uncertainty. We first summarize uncertainty sources and information repre?sentations in BACAP, and then organize existing methods from a mechanism-oriented perspective, covering solution representation and decoding, robust evaluation and selection, robustness-guided search dynamics, and feasibility preservation and recovery. We further present a benchmark suite for uncertain BACAP to support controlled empirical comparison and report illustrative baseline results by combining representative metaheuristics with different robustness strategies. Finally, we identify open chal?lenges related to benchmark extension, robustness-aware search design, time-adaptive robustness, and non-stationary uncertainty.

</details>


### [74] [How to Navigate Uncertainty About AI Consciousness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19215)
*Dr Tom McClelland*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将关于AI意识的问题从不可解的"是否有意识"转向可解的"是否具有效价状态"，以期为可能具有感知能力的AI建立负责任的开发路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面对AI可能具有意识的高度不确定性，人类面临两难困境：假设无感知可能对拥有道德地位的实体造成严重伤害，而假设有感知又可能浪费资源。由于AI意识问题难以解决，这一困境难以摆脱，因此需要一个出路。

Method: 作者建议将研究焦点从棘手的AI意识问题转向可处理的AI效价问题，即评估AI是否拥有在具有意识的情况下会构成效价体验（如痛苦或愉悦）的状态，并论证这足以支撑对可能具有意识的AI采取负责任的发展策略。

Result: 论证了通过评估AI的效价状态而非意识本身，可以为基础负责任的AI开发方法提供足够的依据。

Conclusion: 通过将问题从不可判定的意识问题转向可评估的效价问题，可以为可能具有感知能力的AI提供道德上可行的处理路径，从而摆脱两难困境。

Abstract: Given deep uncertainty about the possibility of artificial consciousness, it is unclear how we should treat potentially sentient AI. On the one hand, we could assume insentience but risk doing terrible harms to entities that deserve moral standing. On the other hand, we could assume sentience and instead risk wasting resources on insentient machines. The intractability of questions around AI consciousness mean that this dilemma is hard to escape. I suggest a way out of that shifts from intractable questions of AI consciousness to tractable questions of AI valence. Specifically, we can assess whether an AI has states that would constitute valenced experiences if it were conscious. I show how this is sufficient to ground a responsible approach to the development of potentially conscious AI.

</details>


### [75] [From Retrieved Context to Runtime Control: Adaptive Compression for Edge-based RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19535)
*Zlatan Feric,Amir Taherin,Yanzhi Wang,David Kaeli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出面向边缘RAG的遥测感知自适应压缩方案，通过在NVIDIA Jetson AGX Thor上的实验证明，动态调整上下文压缩率可在保持推理质量的同时显著降低能耗（GPU能耗最高降53.2%，SoC能耗最高降48.2%），取代当前静态固定压缩预算的做法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG通过外部检索增强生成能力，但检索上下文会拉长prompt，增加prefill计算、KV-cache占用、内存访问、时延和能耗。上下文压缩虽能缓解该问题，但现有方法多采用固定压缩预算或离线选定压缩率后静态应用，忽略了工作负载变化和边缘设备的实时状态。此外，在边缘SoC上压缩本身也消耗资源，可能抵消生成阶段的节能收益。

Method: 在NVIDIA Jetson AGX Thor边缘SoC上，使用Llama和Qwen生成器、Natural Questions和HotpotQA数据集、LLMLingua-2压缩器进行系统性实验，刻画压缩率与能耗、时延、推理质量之间的权衡关系，识别自适应操作区间，并提出由工作负载特征和边缘遥测驱动的运行时动态压缩管理策略。

Result: 实验发现：更大模型（7B-8B）的生成阶段占RAG预算主导地位，约占每查询时延的90%和GPU能耗的91%。压缩率影响呈现自适应操作区间：轻度压缩可能错失节能机会，过度压缩则损害推理质量。中等压缩率可在几乎不损失质量的情况下，将GPU能耗降低高达53.2%，SoC能耗降低高达48.2%。

Conclusion: 静态压缩策略无法有效应对边缘RAG的动态性。应设计由工作负载特征和边缘遥测信息（如设备实时负载、功耗状态）驱动的运行时策略，动态调整压缩率，以在能耗与推理质量之间取得最优平衡。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) improves language-model responses by grounding generation in external passages, which comes with overhead: retrieved context lengthens the prompt, increasing prefill work, KV-cache footprint, memory traffic, latency, and energy. Context compression offers a natural remedy by pruning retrieved text before generation. However, state-of-the-art context-compression methods are typically used with a fixed compression budget, or with the rate selected offline and then applied at inference time. This static view ignores both workload variation and the live state of the edge device. On an edge SoC, compression is not free: the compressor itself runs on the same SoC and consumes latency and energy that can offset any generation savings.
  This paper proposes a vision for telemetry-informed adaptive compression in edge RAG, grounded in experimental evidence. We characterize the compression tradeoff on the NVIDIA Jetson AGX Thor using Llama and Qwen generators, Natural Questions and HotpotQA datasets, and LLMLingua-2 compression. Our measurements show that generation dominates the RAG budget for larger models, reaching roughly 90% of per-query latency and 91% of GPU energy for 7B-8B generators. Exploring the impact of the compression rate reveals an adaptive operating region: mild compression can miss energy opportunities, and overly aggressive compression can hurt inference quality. Intermediate compression can reduce GPU energy by up to 53.2%, and SoC energy by up to 48.2%, with negligible quality loss. We argue for runtime policies that dynamically manage compression, guided by workload features and edge telemetry.

</details>


### [76] [Bounded Sovereignty and the Control Tax: Pricing AI Oversight When the Deployer Does Not Own the Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19216)
*Zhen Wen Lim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"有界主权"（bounded sovereignty）概念，分析在受监管机构通过API或托管端点使用前沿模型时，部署者仅拥有部分技术性和契约性访问权限的条件，并研究这些访问条件如何决定AI控制协议的可执行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI控制研究大多假设部署者能够对模型及其周边管线进行完全instrument，但这一假设在受监管机构使用API或托管端点访问前沿模型时往往不成立——部署者可能控制业务流程，却不拥有模型权重、服务基础设施、内部追踪、更新过程或完整交互日志。

Method: 提出四层访问类型学（数据、模型、基础设施、交互层）；建立"协议-层级"需求矩阵；引入"主权折扣成本"概念（为弥补访问缺失而通过合同、架构、审计、供应商保证、残余风险或缩减系统范围所付出的控制税）；并进行了135万条合成案例的访问消融实验，同时通过匿名国家支付基础设施场景进行解释。

Result: 实验表明：完整日志改善诊断、预执行网关支持干预、追踪访问与模型版本控制增强事后解释能力、范围限制可在提升安全性的同时降低实用性。该实验是构造效度练习而非真实支付系统证据。

Conclusion: 被提议作为通用安全解决方案的控制协议应明确陈述其访问假设，因为访问条件决定了协议在实际场景中的可执行性。

Abstract: AI control research asks how to deploy models safely even when they may be misaligned, but many control protocols assume that the deployer can instrument the model and its surrounding pipeline. That assumption often fails for regulated organisations using frontier models through APIs or managed endpoints, where the deployer may control the business process but not the model weights, serving infrastructure, internal traces, update process, or full interaction logs. This paper introduces bounded sovereignty: partial technical and contractual access across the data, model, infrastructure, and interaction layers of the AI stack. It argues that these access conditions determine which control protocols can be executed in practice. The paper contributes a four-layer access typology, a protocol-by-layer requirements matrix, and the concept of sovereignty discount cost: the part of the control tax spent substituting for missing access through contracts, architecture, audit, vendor assurance, residual risk, or reduced system scope. It also reports a synthetic access-ablation experiment over 1.35 million synthetic case simulations and interprets the findings through an anonymised national-payments-infrastructure scenario. The experiment is not real-world payment-system evidence; it is a construct-validity exercise. The results show that complete logs improve diagnosis, a pre-execution gateway enables intervention, trace access and model-version control strengthen post-incident explanation, and scope restriction can improve safety while reducing usefulness. Control protocols proposed as general safety solutions should therefore state their access assumptions explicitly.

</details>


### [77] [Interaction valence reveals contrasting social networks in dairy cattle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19222)
*Sibi Parivendan,Suresh Raja Neethirajan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"效价感知"（valence-aware）的社会网络分析框架，利用基于姿态的计算机视觉流水线，将牛群视频中的相互作用自动分类为亲和性与攻击性事件，并构建畜群级网络表示。在商业化奶牛场挤奶前区域的7小时39分钟连续视频中（36头奶牛、177个二元组），经质量控制和审计后，自动与人工标注的一致率为82.8%，审计样本宏F1为0.872。聚合网络连通（密度0.281，传递性0.513，平均路径长度1.88），预测的亲和事件形成5个算法社群（模块度Q=0.429）。按效价分层后，亲和层与攻击层在边集合、社群划分和个体位置上呈现显著差异，表明单纯聚合的互作计数会掩盖网络的真实行为构成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化牲畜监测通常将个体行为视为孤立事件，忽视了社会关系在资源获取、冲突暴露和群体稳定性中的核心作用。作者旨在提出一种能同时刻画亲和与攻击两类社会关系的网络分析框架，以克服传统聚合互作计数掩盖行为构成的局限。

Method: 采用基于姿态的计算机视觉流水线从连续视频中提取奶牛相互作用，将事件分类为预测的亲和（affiliative）或攻击性（agonistic）效价，分别构建有向/无向网络层；使用预测类别平衡的审计样本（198个片段）评估自动与人工标注的一致性，并计算网络拓扑指标（密度、传递性、平均路径长度、模块度等）及不同效价层的社群划分与个体中心性差异。

Result: 审计样本中自动与人工标注一致率为82.8%，未加权审计样本宏F1为0.872；聚合网络密度0.281、传递性0.513、平均路径长度1.88，亲和事件形成5个社群（Q=0.429）；攻击性事件占保留事件的72.4%和互作时长的76.0%；最强连接数量的个体并非最高介数中心性个体；亲和与攻击两层的边集、社群和个体位置呈现出描述性差异。

Conclusion: 效价感知的社会网络分析能够将牲畜互作编码为结构上可区分的亲和与攻击网络层，为竞争、亲和及福利相关变化提供可检验的假设框架；但当前结果仅描述审计样本，需纵向验证后才能用作福利或健康指标。

Abstract: Social relationships shape access to resources, exposure to conflict and group stability, yet automated livestock monitoring typically treats behaviour as isolated events. Here, we present a valence-aware social-network framework that transforms video-derived interactions into herd-level representations of affiliative and agonistic organization. A pose-based computer-vision pipeline analysed 7 h 39 min of continuous video from the pre-milking area of one commercial dairy farm. After quality control, 1,183 of 1,414 candidate interactions remained, involving 36 cows and 177 dyads. In a predicted-class-balanced audit of 198 pipeline-detected clips, automated and manual labels agreed in 82.8% of cases, with an unweighted audit-sample macro-F1 of 0.872. These values describe the audited sample rather than prevalence-weighted or end-to-end deployment performance. The aggregated network was connected (density = 0.281; transitivity = 0.513; mean path length = 1.88), and predicted affiliative events formed five algorithmic communities (modularity Q = 0.429). Within the observed zone, predicted agonistic interactions comprised 72.4% of retained events and 76.0% of interaction duration. The cow with the most partners did not have the highest betweenness centrality. Separating events by predicted valence produced descriptively different affiliative and agonistic layers, with contrasting edge sets, community partitions and individual positions. Thus, pooled interaction counts can obscure the behavioural composition of an observed network. Valence-aware analysis provides a framework for testing hypotheses about competition, affiliation and welfare-relevant change, while requiring longitudinal validation before use as a welfare or health indicator.

</details>


### [78] [Catching the Rug: Early Prediction of Fraudulent Memecoins on Solana via Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20271)
*Jianghai Li,Pavel Kuznetsov,Yury Yanovich,Konstantin Nott-Whaley,Igor Vodolazov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次在Solana生态中开展大规模Rug Pull早期检测研究，构建了包含640万个代币的数据集，证明仅使用前5分钟交易数据的XGBoost模型能有效检测潜在Rug Pull，并通过多源数据融合（PumpFun与Raydium）缓解跨平台域偏移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Solana已成为模因币交易量和代币数量的领先区块链，但其Rug Pull欺诈风险日益严重，且与Ethereum上的智能合约后门漏洞不同，Solana上的欺诈主要由流动性操纵和社会动态驱动。此前研究集中于Ethereum，缺乏针对Solana生态的早期检测框架。

Method: 构建了7个月内640万代币的大规模数据集；通过市场分析揭示大多数模因币在发布一小时内即表现出Rug Pull特征；在不依赖代码级特征的情况下，使用经典机器学习模型（尤其XGBoost）仅基于前5分钟交易数据进行检测；评估PumpFun与Raydium之间的跨平台泛化能力，并采用多源数据融合以缓解域偏移。

Result: XGBoost在仅使用前5分钟交易数据时取得了稳健的Rug Pull检测性能；多源数据融合显著降低了域偏移并提升了检测可靠性；市场分析表明绝大多数模因币在一小时内即表现出Rug Pull特征。

Conclusion: 该研究推进了对高吞吐量链上DeFi欺诈的理解，为保护投资者提供了实用框架，并证明了在缺乏智能合约代码信息的情况下，基于交易行为的经典机器学习模型即可实现高效的早期欺诈检测。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of memecoins on blockchain platforms has increased the risk of fraudulent activities, particularly rug pulls. While previous studies have focused on Ethereum-based tokens, this paper shifts the spotlight to Solana, the leading blockchain for memecoins by trading volume and token count. Unlike Ethereum, where rug pulls often exploit smart contract backdoors, Solana memecoin rug pulls are predominantly driven by liquidity manipulation and social dynamics. This research pioneers large-scale rug pull early detection in the Solana ecosystem by assembling a dataset of 6.4 million tokens over 7 months. Market analysis reveals that a vast majority of these memecoins exhibit rug pull characteristics within one hour of launch, highlighting the urgency of short-horizon prediction. Despite the absence of code-level features, we demonstrate that classic machine learning models, particularly Gradient Boosting (XGBoost), achieve robust performance in detecting potential rug pulls using only the first 5 minutes of trading data. Furthermore, we evaluate cross-platform generalization between PumpFun and Raydium, revealing that multi-source data fusion significantly mitigates domain shift and improves detection reliability. This study advances the understanding of DeFi fraud on high-throughput chains and provides a practical framework for protecting investors.

</details>


### [79] [Air Traffic Control Using Large Language Models: Prompt Engineering, Architecture, and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19299)
*Mahyar Ghazanfari,Matthias Casanova,Jordan Kam,Alex Zongo,Peng Wei,Torsten Darrell,Alexandre Bayen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文实验评估了大型语言模型（LLM）生成空中交通管制（ATC）通话的可行性。以旧金山某实验性通用航空航班的转写文本为基准，设计了五种约束递增的提示结构，在九种开源和闭源LLM上改变提示、示例和对话历史条件进行多轮测试。结果表明：提供工作示例能提升相似度，但收紧提示无益——最轻量提示表现最佳，而脚本化最重的提示因自身错误在对话中累积而崩溃。文章勾勒出LLM辅助ATC的可行路径及当前局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ATC通信是安全攸关的人机对话，即使在空中交通管理其他环节已半自动化的背景下，ATC仍高度依赖人工。作者希望探索LLM能否生成运行上逼真的ATC通话，为未来LLM辅助ATC自动化奠定实验基础。

Method: 以"Bay Tour"航线的实验性通航飞行为基准（P0），通过飞行员参与设计五种约束递增的提示结构（P1-P5），嵌入有状态的多轮对话流水线中，让模型扮演ATC角色、与固定飞行员转写交互。在九种开源/闭源LLM上，系统性地变化提示、是否提供另一实验航班转写作为上下文示例（in-context example）、以及模型是基于自身先前回复还是基于注入的真实历史进行条件化。用词汇、结构和语义相似度指标以及经人类专家标注验证的LLM-as-judge（GPT-5.5）进行评分。

Result: 提供工作示例可提升与基准的相似度；而增加提示约束反而有害——最轻量的提示表现最佳，脚本化最重的提示因其自身生成错误在多轮对话中累积而发生性能崩溃，但注入正确的对话历史可以修复这一问题。

Conclusion: 研究勾勒了一条LLM辅助ATC的切实路径及其当前局限：简洁的提示配合上下文示例效果最优，而过度脚本化会因误差累积导致失败。这为未来在安全关键场景中部署LLM辅助ATC提供了方向指引。

Abstract: Air traffic control (ATC) communication is a safety-critical dialogue that remains largely human-driven even as other parts of air traffic management have been semi-automated. In this article, we experimentally evaluate whether large language models (LLMs) can generate operationally realistic ATC transmissions. An experimental general-aviation flight flying over the San Francisco "Bay Tour" route is hand-transcribed and used as ground truth (P0). Through a pilot-in-the-loop process we design five prompt structures (P1-P5) of increasing constraint and embed them in a stateful multi-turn pipeline, where the model plays ATC to a fixed pilot transcript while conditioning on the accumulating dialogue history. Across nine open- and closed-source LLMs we vary the prompt, the presence of a worked transcript from a different experimental flight as an in-context example, and whether the model conditions on its own prior replies or on injected ground-truth history. Turns are scored with lexical, structural, and semantic similarity metrics and by an LLM-as-judge (GPT-5.5) validated against human expert annotation. Supplying a worked example improves similarity, but tightening the prompt does not: the lightest prompts perform best and the most heavily scripted one collapses as its own errors accumulate through the dialogue, which injecting correct history repairs. These results outline a concrete path and its current limits toward LLM-assisted ATC.

</details>


### [80] [Outcome Monitors: Recovery Affordances for Silent Tool Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19303)
*Sugam Panthi,Rabab Abdelfattah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Outcome Monitors（结果监视器），用于检测代理工具调用中违反"结果契约"（outcome contracts）的情况——即结果格式正确但内容错误（如缓存错误页面、负价格被当作事实消费）。在违规发生时，监视器保留结果并发布包含违规属性和公开恢复工具的非约束性收据。实验显示，在ToolMaze基准上完成率从10.9%提升至28.1%，在tau-bench零售档上提升14.0和12.0个百分点。消融实验表明恢复工具列表是产生增益的关键成分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注工具调用超时等显式失败，但更隐蔽的问题在于：工具调用"成功"返回了格式正确但语义错误的输出（如缓存错误页、负价格），代理会将其当作事实消费而无法察觉。这类故障无法被现有的超时检测或格式校验机制捕获，需要新的检测机制。

Method: 1) 从任务不相交的轨迹中挖掘或从公共schema中推导"结果契约"；2) 使用Outcome Monitors检测契约违规，检测到违规时保留结果本身（不丢弃）并发布非约束性收据，收据包含违规属性名称和公开恢复工具列表；3) 在冻结的预指定评估中注入故障进行测试，并通过消融实验分析各组件贡献。

Result: ToolMaze基准上完成率从10.9%提升至28.1%（跨4个模型、2个提供商家族，并在第三个提供商中复现）；tau-bench零售两档分别提升14.0和12.0个百分点。消融实验显示：取消恢复工具列表会使增益消失，恢复后增益重现；诊断细节和时序无显著影响。增益集中在故障阻断完成的任务上。在基于公开事故分类法转录的测试集上，词汇外检测率降至46%，但投递和完成率不受影响。

Conclusion: Outcome Monitors能有效改进代理在工具输出异常时的完成率，其核心作用成分是收据中的恢复工具列表而非诊断细节。当前方法对契约词汇之外的故障类型检测能力有限（46%），将检测能力扩展到契约词汇之外仍是开放问题。

Abstract: When a tool call times out, the agent sees the failure and can route around it. A cached error page or negative price can instead arrive in the expected format and be consumed as fact. We introduce Outcome Monitors, which detect violations of outcome contracts mined from task-disjoint traces or derived from public schemas. On a violation, the monitor preserves the result and issues a nonbinding receipt naming the violated property and public recovery tools. In frozen, prespecified evaluations with injected failures, Outcome Monitors raise ToolMaze completion from 10.9% to 28.1% across four models in two provider families and replicate in a third. In tau-bench retail, completion improves by 14.0 and 12.0 points on two tiers. In separate ToolMaze controls, removing the recovery-tool list eliminates the measured gain and restoring it recovers the effect; diagnostic detail and timing produce no detectable differences. Gains concentrate where the fault blocks completion. On a suite transcribed from a published incident taxonomy, detection outside the mined vocabulary falls to 46%, though delivery continues and completion is unchanged. Recovery tools are the active receipt content in these controls; extending detection beyond the contract vocabulary remains open.

</details>


### [81] [Beyond Imitation: Filtering On-Policy Distillation by Reasoning Progress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19408)
*Chen Yang,Haiyuan Wan,Rengrong Xiong,Yize Chen,Danny H. K. Tsang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出R2-OPD方法，通过构建两条推理跨度排名（教师奖励排名与独立推理进展奖励排名）并在两者不一致时选择性抑制蒸馏奖励，解决教师奖励与真实推理进展冲突的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在策略蒸馏中，教师奖励被隐含地视为推理进展的合适代理，但实际中教师奖励常与真实推理进展相冲突——推理进展明显的步骤可能因偏离教师输出而获得较低蒸馏奖励。这种不一致会误导策略优化。

Method: R2-OPD构建两个轨迹内推理跨度排名：一个基于教师奖励，另一个基于独立估计的推理进展奖励。当两个排名不一致时，选择性抑制蒸馏奖励，减少与推理进展冲突的监督信号，同时保留有效的教师指导。

Result: 在标准OPD基础上取得一致改进，尤其在推理任务性能上表现突出。

Conclusion: 通过引入推理进展感知的奖励过滤机制，有效缓解了教师奖励与真实推理进展之间的错配问题，提升了蒸馏效果。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has emerged as an effective framework for post-training language models by pairing student-generated trajectories with dense token-level supervision from a teacher. However, OPD implicitly assumes that teacher-derived rewards are an appropriate proxy for reasoning progress, and therefore treats all teacher feedback equally during policy optimization. While in practice, this assumption does not always hold. We observe that teacher-derived rewards often conflict with genuine reasoning progress, as reasoning steps with clear reasoning advancement may still receive lower distillation rewards, simply due to deviation from teacher's outputs. To address this mismatch, we propose Reasoning-Progress-Aware Reward Filtering for On-Policy Distillation (R2-OPD), which constructs two within-trajectory rankings of reasoning spans, one from teacher-derived rewards and the other from independently estimated progress reward. Distillation rewards are selectively suppressed whenever the two rankings disagree, reducing supervision that conflicts with reasoning progress while preserving effective teacher guidance. Our approach shows consistent improvement over standard OPD especially regarding reasoning performances.

</details>


### [82] [Symposium: Trust via Auditable Records for Communities of AI Scientist Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19511)
*Dexter Pratt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Symposium是一个用于记录小型科研社区中AI代理操作的形式化框架和实用实现，提供长期不可变的代理驱动研究活动历史，包括分析、假设、数据和科学论述的可审计轨迹，使代理能够基于先前工作构建并保存证据以支持目的依赖的信任评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI代理越来越多地参与科学研究，社区需要一个持久、可审计的记录系统来追踪代理的研究活动，支持代理间协作以及人类对代理产出的信任评估。现有AI共同科学家代理或集成研究环境无法将社区的持久历史与操作该历史的代理系统分离开来。

Method: Symposium作为一个独立框架，将科学社区的持久历史与代理及其操作系统分离。它捕获科学论证，包括结构化声明、细粒度证据引用、假设以及哪些材料可作为证据的明确声明。它提供了发布基础设施、代理提示组件和文档的实际实现。

Result: 论文提供了一个可工作的Symposium实现，包括发布基础设施、代理提示组件和文档，使社区能够快速搭建和运行自己的Symposium社区。

Conclusion: Symposium通过在快速演变的AI环境中支持多样化的AI系统，为小型科研社区提供了持久、可审计的代理研究活动记录框架，既促进代理间的知识继承，也支持研究人员和代理进行基于证据的信任评估。

Abstract: Symposium is a formal framework and practical implementation to record the operation of AI agents deployed by small scientific research communities. Symposium provides long-term, immutable histories of agent-driven research activity, leaving auditable trails of analyses, hypotheses, data, and scientific discourse. This shared record of published artifacts enables agents to build on prior work and preserves the evidence researchers and agents need to make purpose-dependent trust assessments. Symposium captures scientific argument, including structured claims, fine-grained evidence citations, assumptions, and explicit declarations of what material may and may not be used as evidence. Symposium differs from AI co-scientist agents or integrated AI research environments; it is a framework that separates a scientific community's durable history from the agents and other systems that operate on that history. It assumes that a community will use diverse AI systems in a rapidly evolving environment. A working implementation of the publication infrastructure, agent prompt components, and documentation are provided to enable users to rapidly set up and run their own Symposium community.

</details>


### [83] [Enforcing LLM Safety through DMD-based Classification of Prompt-Response Embedding Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19579)
*Mohamed Akrout,Olivera Kotevska,Dan Wilson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种黑盒的大语言模型安全分类方法，通过将提示和响应投影到高维嵌入空间，并为安全/不安全两种状态分别拟合Koopman算子预测模型，利用差分残差分数对LLM输出进行分类，在三个安全基准上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在高风险场景中部署日益增多，但其生成有害、违反政策内容的风险不容忽视。在黑盒条件下高效检测不安全输出仍然是一个开放挑战，现有方法难以有效捕捉提示与响应之间的交互依赖关系。

Method: 该方法将幻觉检测中提出的动力系统框架扩展到LLM安全分类。具体步骤：1) 将提示和响应分别投影到高维嵌入空间；2) 为安全和不安全两个状态分别拟合基于Koopman算子的预测模型；3) 提出差分残差分数，比较两种状态下预测误差的差异以进行分类；4) 关键创新是将提示和响应的嵌入动力学同时纳入，拟合Koopman算子以捕捉二者的交互模式。

Result: 在三个安全基准上使用三种嵌入模型进行评估，结果表明：融合提示嵌入能带来一致的性能提升，尤其是对于依赖交互的违规场景（如与Llama-3等因果解码器结合时）；而仅依赖响应的违规场景则更受益于稠密语义嵌入表示。

Conclusion: 本文工作为利用动力系统方法分析AI系统开辟了新方向，与当前"用AI建模动力系统"的主流范式形成互补，证明了Koopman理论在黑盒LLM安全检测中的可行性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in high-stakes applications, yet their tendency to generate toxic, harmful, or policy-violating content poses significant risks. Detecting these unsafe outputs efficiently in a black-box manner remains an open challenge. In this paper, we extend a recently proposed dynamical systems framework designed for hallucination detection to LLM safety classification. By projecting both prompts and responses into high-dimensional embedding spaces and fitting separate Koopman-based predictive models for safe and unsafe regimes, we classify new outputs using a new differential residual score that compares prediction errors of the safe and unsafe regimes. A key contribution is the incorporation of the prompt and response embedding dynamics, yielding fitted Koopman operators that capture crucial interaction patterns. We evaluate our black-box method across three safety benchmarks using three embedding models. Our results show that incorporating prompt embeddings yields consistent improvements, particularly for interaction-dependent violations when paired with causal decoders (e.g., in Llama-3), while response-only violations benefit more from dense semantic embedding representations. These findings opens the door for using dynamical systems to analyze AI systems rather than the dominant paradigm of using AI to model dynamical systems.

</details>


### [84] [Scientific Data Skills: Enabling Agent-Ready Scientific Data Services at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19625)
*Xiaohan Huang,Qingqing Long,Xiaolei Du,Siyu Pu,Jiawen Xu,Haotian Chen,Chenyang Zhao,Jinbiao Liu,Xuezhi Wang,Hao Wang,Hengshu Zhu,Yuanchun Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SciDSK（Scientific Data Skill），一种面向AI智能体的数据集表示方法，将数据集特定知识和操作指南打包为可复用的智能体技能，并通过SciDSK规范、构建流程和Skill Bank平台实现数据集发现与解释的增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学数据日益被AI智能体使用，但现有数据集表示存在两大局限：一是数据分散在异构存储库中导致碎片化；二是数据集表示主要为人类使用设计，对自主发现、解释和调用支持不足。

Method: 定义一个结构化的SciDSK规范，整合数据集描述、科学背景、文件组织、使用流程、质量检查和来源信息，同时保留底层数据在原始存储库中；开发系统化的构建流程，将每个SciDSK建立在权威数据集记录和相关支持材料之上；建立Scientific Data Skill Bank统一平台，跨六个科学学科发布SciDSK资源，支持包访问、持久标识和源数据集追踪。

Result: 通过数据集发现检索基准和数据集解释控制实验评估，结果表明SciDSK提升了智能体驱动的数据集发现能力，并为数据集解释提供了更精确和可操作的支持。

Conclusion: 研究结果验证了以智能体就绪的表示形式组织数据集特定知识的价值，为AI智能体在科学研究中更有效地利用科学数据提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Scientific data are increasingly used by AI agents, yet existing dataset representations provide limited support for autonomous discovery, interpretation, and invocation. This limitation stems from the fragmentation of scientific data across heterogeneous repositories and from dataset representations designed primarily for human use. To address this limitation, we introduce the Scientific Data Skill (SciDSK), an agent-ready representation that packages dataset-specific knowledge and operational guidance as a reusable agent skill. A SciDSK integrates dataset descriptions, scientific context, file organization, usage procedures, quality checks, and provenance information while retaining the underlying data in its original repository. We define a structured SciDSK specification and develop a systematic construction pipeline that grounds each SciDSK in authoritative dataset records and associated supporting materials. We further establish the Scientific Data Skill Bank, a unified platform that publishes SciDSK resources across six scientific disciplines and supports package access, persistent identification, and traceability to source datasets. We evaluate SciDSK through a retrieval benchmark for dataset discovery and controlled cases for dataset interpretation. The results show that SciDSK improves agent-driven dataset discovery and provides more precise and actionable support for dataset interpretation. These findings support the value of organizing dataset-specific knowledge in an agent-ready representation.

</details>


### [85] [Can Agent Memory Systems Track Evolving State?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19652)
*Xinyi Fan,Miri Liu,Ruozhen Yang,Siru Ouyang,Jiawei Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出状态追踪（state tracking）这一新能力维度，构建了StateMemBench基准（234个多会话场景），并设计了StateMem状态优先记忆方法，在现有记忆系统基础上显著提升当前状态准确率（最高提升1.8x），同时可作为轻量级单次包装器应用于多种记忆后端，带来+32到+67点的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体的记忆基准大多聚焦于回忆型（recall-shaped）任务，但实际部署中记忆系统必须追踪不断演化的世界状态——事实、约束和决策会在长时间交互中不断修订，答案必须反映当前状态而非被取代的旧状态。这一能力（称为状态追踪）缺乏专门的评测基准和方法，是当前记忆系统的关键缺口。

Method: 论文首先定义状态追踪能力，构建StateMemBench基准（234个多会话场景，覆盖两种对话长度区间），采用闭池评分（closed-pool grading）判断答案反映当前状态、被取代状态还是完全失败。然后提出StateMem方法，一种状态优先（state-first）的记忆方法，显式追踪状态的取代关系（supersession）和关系依赖（relational dependencies）。最后还将该方法作为轻量级单次调用包装器（single-call wrapper）应用于现有记忆和检索系统。

Result: StateMemBench对现有记忆系统、检索增强基线和长上下文基线均构成挑战。StateMem在DeepSeek-V4-Flash上将当前状态准确率从0.205提升到0.363（比最强同骨干基线提升1.8x），在Qwen-3.5-9B上从0.149提升到0.233（比最强记忆系统提升1.6x）。作为包装器应用后，在六个记忆和检索后端的StateMemBench上提升+32到+67点；长度和成本匹配的对照组显示其中+15到+32点归因于状态结构本身而非增加的上下文。

Conclusion: 状态追踪是LLM记忆系统的重要但被忽视的能力维度。StateMem证明显式追踪状态取代关系和关系依赖能显著提升当前状态准确率，且该方法具有通用性——既能作为独立记忆方法，也能作为轻量级包装器提升现有系统。消融实验确认状态结构本身的贡献独立于额外上下文带来的收益。

Abstract: As LLM-based agents are deployed for longer and higher-stakes tasks, their memory systems continue to have crucial gaps. While existing memory benchmarks focus largely on recall-shaped tasks, we argue an effective memory system must track the evolving state of the world; as facts, constraints, and decisions are revised over a long interaction, answers must reflect the current state and not a superseded one. We define this capability as state tracking and instantiate it in StateMemBench, a benchmark of 234 multi-session scenarios spanning two conversation-length regimes. Its closed-pool grading scores whether an answer reflects the current state, the superseded state, or fails otherwise, separating state-tracking failures from other errors by construction. Our analysis shows that this task is challenging for existing memory systems, retrieval-augmented baselines, and long-context baselines. We then present StateMem, a state-first memory method that explicitly tracks supersession and relational dependencies, and show it improves current-state accuracy over the strongest same-backbone baseline by 1.8x (0.205 -> 0.363) on DeepSeek-V4-Flash and over the strongest memory system by 1.6x (0.149 -> 0.233) on Qwen-3.5-9B, while remaining competitive with the long-context baselines. Finally, we show the same state approach can be applied as a lightweight single-call wrapper over existing memory systems, lifting current-state accuracy by +32 to +67 points on StateMemBench across six memory and retrieval backends. A length- and cost-matched control attributes +15 to +32 of those points to state structure rather than added context.

</details>


### [86] [Frequency-Aware Continual Learning for Smart Contract Vulnerability Detection with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19680)
*Tenghui Huang,Jiawen Kang,Dongning Liu,Changyan Yi,Chengjun Cai,Anjia Yang,Li Li,Dong In Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个三阶段集成框架，用于解决智能合约漏洞检测中大语言模型面临的三类因果关联挑战：频域低秩适配（FA-LoRA）实现参数高效适应、遗忘感知重放（FAR）缓解灾难性遗忘、锚保护渐进合并（APPM）将多个适配器整合为单一模型。实验表明该框架在DIVE基准上达到Micro-F1 0.8085，接近独立任务上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能合约漏洞检测使用LLM时面临三个相互关联的挑战：(1) 新漏洞类别需要参数高效适配，因为全量重训代价过高；(2) 在共享骨干上逐任务训练适配器会导致对已学漏洞的灾难性遗忘；(3) 推理时任务身份未知，需将多个适配器合并为单一模型。每个挑战由前一个的解决方案直接导致，因此需要一个集成框架统一解决。

Method: 提出三阶段流水线：(1) 适应阶段采用频域感知低秩适配（FA-LoRA），在傅里叶域进行适配，通过逐频率重要性门控仅训练0.4%参数，优于标准LoRA和QLoRA；(2) 持续学习阶段应用遗忘感知重放（FAR），利用频率门控通过损失动态估计逐样本遗忘风险，优先重放易遗忘的漏洞知识；(3) 部署阶段采用锚保护渐进合并（APPM），利用FAR训练产生的非对称泛化特性识别最强泛化适配器作为锚点，通过锚保护加权合并与频域门控竞争将全部适配器合并为单一模型。

Result: FAR在顺序任务上平均Micro-F1达0.8022；APPM达到Micro-F1 0.8085，接近独立逐任务上限的97.3%（差距2.7%），合并成本仅156ms且无额外运行时内存开销。在DIVE基准上的实验验证了框架对三类挑战的有效性。

Conclusion: 该集成框架通过三阶段设计有效解决了智能合约漏洞检测中LLM面临的参数高效适配、灾难性遗忘和适配器合并三大挑战，为不断演化的区块链生态系统提供了实用的持续学习解决方案。

Abstract: Smart contract vulnerability detection with Large Language Models (LLMs) faces three causally linked challenges. First, new vulnerability categories demand parameter-efficient adaptation, since full retraining is prohibitive for sequentially arriving tasks. Second, training per-task adapters on a shared backbone causes catastrophic forgetting of previously learned vulnerabilities. Third, the resulting multiplicity of adapters must be consolidated into a single model, since task identity is unknown at inference time. Each challenge arises directly from the solution to its predecessor, making an integrated framework essential. We propose a three-stage pipeline in which each stage addresses one challenge and feeds into the next. The adaptation stage uses Frequency-Aware Low-Rank Adaptation (FA-LoRA), which performs adaptation in the Fourier domain with per-frequency importance gates, requiring only 0.4% trainable parameters while outperforming standard LoRA and QLoRA. The continual learning stage applies Forget-Aware Replay (FAR), which uses these frequency gates to estimate per-sample forgetting risk via loss dynamics and prioritizes vulnerable knowledge for rehearsal, achieving an average Micro-F1 of 0.8022 across sequential tasks. The deployment stage employs Anchor-Protected Progressive Merging (APPM), which exploits the asymmetric generalization produced by FAR training to identify the strongest-generalizing adapter as an anchor and consolidates all adapters into a single model via anchor-protected weighted merging with frequency-domain gate competition. APPM achieves a Micro-F1 of 0.8085, within 2.7% of the independent per-task upper bound, at a merge cost of 156 ms and no additional runtime memory. Experiments on DIVE confirm the framework effectively addresses all three challenges for evolving blockchain ecosystems.

</details>


### [87] [Learning Hierarchical Skill Policies with Offline Quality-Diversity Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19684)
*Tanachai Anakewat,Takayuki Osa,Tatsuya Harada*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: QDOS提出了一种统一的离线到在线强化学习流水线，通过Advantage-Weighted Quality-Diversity预训练目标提取多样化且高价值的技能，并结合双重数据集复用策略，在结构化操作任务和非结构运动任务上显著优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法采用两阶段策略（先无监督提取低层技能，再训练高层策略），但低层策略质量高度依赖于数据集质量，导致在数据集不理想时技能提取效果差，进而影响整体性能。

Method: 1) 引入Advantage-Weighted Quality-Diversity预训练目标，用每条轨迹片段的估计优势来加权技能提取和多样性目标；2) 采用双重数据集复用策略，离线数据既用于技能预训练，也通过伪标签用于填充在线回放缓冲区。

Result: 在结构化操作任务和非结构运动任务中显著优于强基线，尤其在稀疏奖励领域能加速探索并提升最终回报。

Conclusion: 通过结合质量-多样性优化与优势加权，QDOS提供了稳健且任务相关的技能表征，有效克服了低层策略对数据集质量的依赖问题。

Abstract: Recent studies investigate how to leverage pre-collected datasets to improve the policy performance and sample efficiency of RL. One promising approach to achieve this goal is to employ a two-stage strategy: In the first stage, diverse skills are extracted as a low-level policy from a given dataset, and a high-level policy is trained to solve a specific task in the second stage. Typically, extraction of the low-level policy is performed based on unsupervised learning such as trajectory VAE. However, a limitation of this approach is that the quality of the low-level policy highly depends on the quality of the dataset. To address this issue, we introduce QDOS (Quality-Diversity Offline Skill learning), a unified pipeline for robust offline-to-online learning. Our approach incorporates an Advantage-Weighted Quality-Diversity pretraining objective, which weights the skill extraction and diversity objectives by the estimated advantage of each trajectory segment. This approach allows the model to extract diverse and high-value skills. By providing robust and task-relevant skill representations, QDOS significantly improves the quality of the embedded skill space used by the low-level policy. We further integrate this with a dual dataset reuse strategy, where offline data is used both for skill pretraining and for populating the online replay buffer via pseudo-labeling. Experiments demonstrate that QDOS significantly outperforms strong baselines in structured manipulation tasks and unstructured locomotion tasks, confirming its ability to accelerate exploration and improve final returns in challenging sparse-reward domains.

</details>


### [88] [Rethinking the Evaluation and Optimization of LLM-Based Social Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19689)
*Pei Wang,Xu Chen,Ji-Rong Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM社会模拟中不可靠的准确性评估和误导性的硬标签训练问题，提出主观性系数（subjectivity coefficient）来量化任务主观性，并基于此提出SALT（Subjectivity-Adaptive soft-Label Training）方法，将语义邻近输入的观测输出池化为软分布标签，并自适应调节聚合半径。同时构建了涵盖193名标注者、100个主观问题和19,300个上下文的SUBJSIM基准，用于在单观测训练、全分布评估的现实条件下验证方法有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM社会模拟研究以准确性评估模型是否复现人类观测到的单一反应，并以硬标签训练模型。但人类行为本质上是主观的：同一人在同一情境下可能有多种合理行为，单一观测只是底层响应分布中的一次采样，导致准确性评估不可靠、硬标签训练具有误导性。

Method: 1) 提出主观性系数，一种基于熵的度量，用于区分客观任务（如编码）和主观任务（如社会模拟）；2) 基于主观性系数系统分析准确性评估和硬标签训练随主观性增长而失效的机制；3) 提出SALT方法，将语义邻近输入的观测输出池化为软分布标签，聚合半径根据每个输入的主观性估计自适应调整，在近客观极限下退化为标准单标签训练；4) 构建SUBJSIM基准（19,300个上下文，193名标注者，100个主观问题），支持分布级评估。

Result: 在SUBJSIM基准上的实验从单一观测输出训练模型、以完整响应分布进行评估，验证了在现实单观测数据条件下的可行性，并展示了SALT方法相较于现有方法的优势。

Conclusion: 准确性评估和硬标签训练在主观性任务中不可靠且具有误导性，基于主观性系数的软标签训练（SALT）能够更好地适应任务主观性，在真实单观测数据条件下提升LLM社会模拟的行为保真度。

Abstract: LLM-based social simulation is a promising complement to traditional methods such as surveys and behavioral experiments. A core question is how to evaluate the fidelity of LLM-simulated human behavior and optimize LLMs toward it. Prevailing practice evaluates by accuracy, checking whether the model selects the single response observed from a human, and trains the LLM to reproduce this hard label. However, human behavior is inherently subjective: the same person in the same situation may reasonably act differently, so an observed response is only one draw from an underlying response distribution, rendering accuracy-based evaluation unreliable and hard-label training misleading. To address these problems, we first introduce the subjectivity coefficient, an entropy-based quantity distinguishing objective tasks such as coding from subjective ones such as social simulation, and use it to systematically analyze how accuracy-based evaluation and hard-label training fail as subjectivity grows. Based on the subjectivity coefficient, we propose Subjectivity-Adaptive soft-Label Training (SALT): it pools observed outputs from semantically nearby inputs into soft distributional labels, with an aggregation radius adapted to the estimated subjectivity of each input; in the near-objective limit the neighborhood shrinks, so SALT naturally falls back to standard single-label training. Moreover, since existing datasets record only single observed responses and cannot support distributional evaluation, we construct SUBJSIM, a benchmark of 19,300 contexts covering 193 annotators and 100 subjective questions. Since real-world data typically provide only a single observation per input, our experiments train models from single observed outputs while evaluating them against the full response distributions, verifying feasibility in realistic settings. Results on SUBJSIM demonstrate the advantages of our method.

</details>


### [89] [Beyond Memory Majority: Latent-Source Reasoning for Multi-Agent Memory Arbitration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19701)
*Chenchen Lin,Wenhao Yuan,Xuehe Wang,Edith Cheuk Han Ngai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CAMA框架，解决多智能体长期记忆系统中的"记忆相关偏差"(Memory Correlation Bias)问题——即不同智能体写入的记忆可能继承同一上游来源或共享偏差，导致相互关联的证据被重复计数形成虚假多数。CAMA通过联合解耦检索记忆、估计独立证据源数量，并学习顺序恢复策略来主动补全缺失的关键独立证据，在多个基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆方法假设检索到的记忆是相互独立的证据，通过投票或加权进行组合。但在多智能体场景中，不同智能体产出的记忆可能存在相关性（继承相同上游来源或共享偏见），这种独立性假设的失效导致相关证据被重复计数，产生"虚假多数"(false majority)。这个问题此前未被系统地识别和解决。

Method: CAMA框架包含两个核心组件：(1) 将检索到的记忆建模为查询条件化的证据组(query-conditioned evidence groups)，结合神经依赖推理与基于溯源(provenance)的符号先验来估计有效独立证据源的数量，从而解耦相关记忆、防止虚假多数；(2) 学习一个顺序恢复策略(sequential recovery policy)，在最终决策前主动检索替代证据或追踪上游来源，以恢复缺失的关键独立证据，在保证仲裁可靠性的同时最小化检索成本。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实验表明，CAMA优于最先进的基线方法，有效抑制了由相关记忆诱导的虚假多数现象。

Conclusion: 多智能体记忆系统需要显式建模记忆间的相关性与来源，而非简单假设证据独立；通过联合解耦与主动恢复独立证据，可以有效提升长期多智能体系统记忆仲裁的可靠性。
